$$

# 1.6 从状态空间表达式求传递函数阵

以上介绍了从传递函数求状态空间表达式的问题，即系统的实现问题。本节介绍从状态空间表达式求传递函数（阵）的问题。

# 1.6.1 传递函数（阵）

# 1. 单输入一单输出系统

已知系统的状态空间表达式：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} \dot {x} = A x + b u \\ y = c x + d u \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.62}
$$

式中， $x$ 为 $n$ 维状态矢量； $y$ 和 $u$ 为输出和输入，它们都是标量； $A$ 为 $n \times n$ 方阵； $b$ 为 $n \times 1$ 列阵； $c$ 为 $1 \times n$ 行阵； $d$ 为标量，一般为零。

对式（1.62）进行拉氏变换，并假定初始条件为零，则有：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {X} (s) = (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A}) ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {b} U (s) \\ \boldsymbol {Y} (s) = \boldsymbol {c} \boldsymbol {X} (s) + d U (s) \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.63}
$$

故 $U - X$ 间的传递函数为：

$$
\boldsymbol {W} _ {u x} (s) = \frac {\boldsymbol {X} (s)}{U (s)} = (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A}) ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {b} \tag {1.64}
$$

它是一个 $n \times 1$ 的列阵函数。

$U - Y$ 间的传递函数为：

$$
W (s) = \frac {Y (s)}{U (s)} = c (s I - A) ^ {- 1} b + d \tag {1.65}
$$

它是一个标量。

# 2. 多输入一多输出系统

已知系统的状态空间表达式：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} \dot {x} = A x + B u \\ y = C x + D u \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.66}
$$

式中， $\pmb{u}$ 为 $r\times 1$ 输入列矢量； $\mathcal{V}$ 为 $m\times 1$ 输出列矢量； $\pmb{B}$ 为 $n\times r$ 控制矩阵； $C$ 为 $m\times n$ 输

出矩阵； $D$ 为 $m\times r$ 直接传递阵； $\pmb{x}$ ， $\mathbf{A}$ 为同单变量系统。

同前，对式（1.66）作拉氏变换并认为初始条件为零，得：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {X} (s) = (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A}) ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {B} \boldsymbol {U} (s) \\ \mathbf {Y} (s) = \mathbf {C} (s \mathbf {I} - \mathbf {A}) ^ {- 1} + \mathbf {D} \mathbf {U} (s) \tag {1.67} \\ \end{array}
$$

故 $U - X$ 间的传递函数为：

$$
\boldsymbol {W} _ {u x} (s) = \left(s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A}\right) ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {B} \tag {1.68}
$$

它是一个 $n \times r$ 矩阵函数。

故 $U - Y$ 间的传递函数为：

$$
\boldsymbol {W} (s) = \boldsymbol {C} (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A}) ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {B} + \boldsymbol {D} \tag {1.69}
$$

它是一个 $m \times r$ 矩阵函数，即

$$
\boldsymbol {W} (s) = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} W _ {1 1} (s) & W _ {1 2} (s) & \dots & W _ {1 r} (s) \\ W _ {2 1} (s) & W _ {2 2} (s) & \dots & W _ {2 r} (s) \\ \vdots & \vdots & & \vdots \\ W _ {m 1} (s) & W _ {m 2} (s) & \dots & W _ {m r} (s) \end{array} \right)
$$

其中各元素 $W_{ij}(s)$ 都是标量函数，它表征第 $j$ 个输入对第 $i$ 个输出的传递关系。当 $i \neq j$ 时，意味着不同标号的插入与输出有相互关联，称为有耦合关系，这正是多变量系统的特点。

式（1.69）还可以表示为：

$$
\boldsymbol {W} (s) = \frac {1}{| s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} |} [ \boldsymbol {C} \operatorname {a d j} (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A}) \boldsymbol {B} + \boldsymbol {D} | s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} | ] \tag {1.70}
$$

可以看出， $W(s)$ 的分母，就是系统矩阵 $\mathbf{A}$ 的特征多项式， $W(s)$ 的分子是一个多项式矩阵。

应当指出，同一系统，尽管其状态空间表达式可以作各种非奇异变换而不是唯一的，但它的传递函数阵是不变的。对于已知系统如式（1.66），其传递函数阵为式（1.69）。当做坐标变换，即令 $z = T^{-1}x$ 时，则该系统的状态空间表达式为：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \dot {z} = T ^ {- 1} A T z + T ^ {- 1} B u \tag {1.71} \\ \boldsymbol {y} = C T z + D u \\ \end{array}
$$

那么对应上式的传递函数阵 $\widetilde{W}(s)$ 应为：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \widetilde {W} (s) = C T \left(s I - T ^ {- 1} A T\right) ^ {- 1} T ^ {- 1} B + D = C \left[ T \left(s I - T ^ {- 1} A T\right) T ^ {- 1} \right] ^ {- 1} B + D \\ = C [ T (s I) T ^ {- 1} - T T ^ {- 1} A T T ^ {- 1} ] ^ {- 1} B + D \\ = C (s I - A) ^ {- 1} B + D = W (s) \\ \end{array}
$$

即同一系统，其传递函数阵是唯一的。

# 1.6.2 子系统在各种连接时的传递函数阵

实际的控制系统，往往由多个子系统组合而成，或并联，或串联，或形成反馈连接。现仅以两个子系统作各种连接为例，推导其等效的传递函数阵。

设系统1为：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} \dot {x} _ {1} = A _ {1} x _ {1} + B _ {1} u _ {1} \\ y _ {1} = C _ {1} x _ {1} + D _ {1} u _ {1} \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.72}
$$

简记为：

$$
\sum 1: \left(A _ {1}, B _ {1}, C _ {1}, D _ {1}\right)
$$

设系统2为：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} \dot {\boldsymbol {x}} _ {2} = \boldsymbol {A} _ {2} \boldsymbol {x} _ {2} + \boldsymbol {B} _ {2} \boldsymbol {u} _ {2} \\ \boldsymbol {y} _ {2} = \boldsymbol {C} _ {2} \boldsymbol {x} _ {2} + \boldsymbol {D} _ {2} \boldsymbol {u} _ {2} \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.73}
$$

简记为：

$$
\sum 2: \left(A _ {2}, B _ {2}, C _ {2}, D _ {2}\right)
$$

# 1. 并联连接

所谓并联连接，是指各子系统在相同输入下，组合系统的输出是各子系统输出的代数和，结构简图如图1.20所示。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-05-05/e98daa7a-7f90-4060-9ef4-3ebaca29cd10/d1d34fbda6629d85ed2b81fb5e289b009afef1114933faf8e25fd01ebaa8322c.jpg)



图1.20 并联连接系统结构图


由式（1.72）和式（1.73），并考虑 $u_{1} = u_{2} = u$ ， $y = y_{1} \pm y_{2}$ ，得系统的状态空间表达式：

$$
\binom {\dot {x} _ {1}} {\dot {x} _ {2}} = \binom {A _ {1}} {0} \binom {0} {A _ {2}} \binom {x _ {1}} {x _ {2}} + \binom {B _ {1}} {B _ {2}} u
$$

$$
\mathbf {y} = \left(\boldsymbol {C} _ {1}, \pm \boldsymbol {C} _ {2}\right) \binom {\boldsymbol {x} _ {1}} {\boldsymbol {x} _ {2}} + \left(\boldsymbol {D} _ {1} \pm \boldsymbol {D} _ {2}\right) \boldsymbol {u}
$$

从而系统的传递函数阵为：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {W} (s) = \left(\boldsymbol {C} _ {1}, \quad \pm \boldsymbol {C} _ {2}\right) \binom {\left(s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {1}\right) ^ {- 1}} {0} \binom {0} {\left(s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {2}\right) ^ {- 1}} \binom {\boldsymbol {B} _ {1}} {\boldsymbol {B} _ {2}} + \left(\boldsymbol {D} _ {1} \pm \boldsymbol {D} _ {2}\right) \\ = \left[ C _ {1} \left(s I - A _ {1}\right) ^ {- 1} B _ {1} + D _ {1} \right] \pm \left[ C _ {2} \left(s I - A _ {2}\right) ^ {- 1} B _ {2} + D _ {2} \right] \tag {1.74} \\ = W _ {1} (s) \pm W _ {2} (s) \\ \end{array}
$$

故子系统并联时，系统传递函数阵等于子系统传递函数阵的代数和。

# 2. 串联连接

串联连接如图1.21所示。读者可自己证明，其串联连接传递函数阵为：

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-05-05/e98daa7a-7f90-4060-9ef4-3ebaca29cd10/fee519f389779b4d6c80312ec2ddc3236c057d05441437505c0f8ad9a26c7d43.jpg)



图1.21 串联连接系统结构图


$$
\boldsymbol {W} (s) = \boldsymbol {W} _ {2} (s) \boldsymbol {W} _ {1} (s) \tag {1.75}
$$

即子系统串联时，系统传递函数阵等于子系统传递函数阵之积。但应注意，传递函数阵相乘，先后次序不能颠倒。

# 3. 具有输出反馈的系统

如图1.22所示，由图可得：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \dot {\boldsymbol {x}} _ {1} = \boldsymbol {A} _ {1} \boldsymbol {x} _ {1} + \boldsymbol {B} _ {1} (\boldsymbol {u} - \boldsymbol {C} _ {2} \boldsymbol {x} _ {2}) \\ \dot {\boldsymbol {x}} _ {2} = \boldsymbol {A} _ {2} \boldsymbol {x} _ {2} + \boldsymbol {B} _ {2} \boldsymbol {C} _ {1} \boldsymbol {x} _ {1} \\ y = y _ {1} = C _ {1} x \\ \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-05-05/e98daa7a-7f90-4060-9ef4-3ebaca29cd10/692b379b60c029fcb1f5f1506d716dd14da1299f73e60ff2aa806adf771a9489.jpg)



图1.22 反馈系统模拟图


即

$$
\begin{array}{l} \binom {\dot {x} _ {1}} {\dot {x} _ {2}} = \binom {A _ {1}} {B _ {2} C _ {1}} \binom {B _ {1} C _ {2}} {A _ {2}} \binom {x _ {1}} {x _ {2}} + \binom {B _ {1}} {0} u \\ \mathbf {y} = \left( \begin{array}{l l} C _ {1}, & 0 \end{array} \right) \binom {\mathbf {x} _ {1}} {\mathbf {x} _ {2}} \\ \end{array}
$$

从而系统的传递函数阵为：

$$
\boldsymbol {W} (s) = \left(\boldsymbol {C} _ {1}, 0\right) \binom {s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {1}} {- \boldsymbol {B} _ {2} \boldsymbol {C} _ {1}} \binom {\boldsymbol {B} _ {1} \boldsymbol {C} _ {2}} {s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {2}} ^ {- 1} \binom {\boldsymbol {B} _ {1}} {0}
$$

这里又遇到分块求逆的问题，假定：

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c} s I - A _ {1} & B _ {1} C _ {2} \\ - B _ {2} C _ {1} & s I - A _ {2} \end{array} \right) ^ {- 1} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} F _ {1 1} & F _ {1 2} \\ F _ {2 1} & F _ {2 2} \end{array} \right)
$$

故有：

$$
\left( \begin{array}{l l} \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 1} & \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 2} \\ \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 1} & \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 2} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l l} s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {1} & \boldsymbol {B} _ {1} \boldsymbol {C} _ {2} \\ - \boldsymbol {B} _ {2} \boldsymbol {C} _ {1} & s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {2} \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{l l} \boldsymbol {I} & \boldsymbol {0} \\ \boldsymbol {0} & \boldsymbol {I} \end{array} \right)
$$

从而得：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 1} (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {1}) - \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 2} \boldsymbol {B} _ {2} \boldsymbol {C} _ {1} = \boldsymbol {I} \\ \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 1} \boldsymbol {B} _ {1} \boldsymbol {C} _ {2} + \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 2} (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {2}) = 0 \\ \end{array}
$$

由上两式解得：

$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {1 1} (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {1}) = \boldsymbol {I} + \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 2} \boldsymbol {B} _ {2} \boldsymbol {C} _ {1} = \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 1} \boldsymbol {B} _ {1} \boldsymbol {C} _ {2} (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {2}) ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {B} _ {2} \boldsymbol {C} _ {1}
$$

即

$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {1 1} = (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {1}) ^ {- 1} - \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 1} \boldsymbol {B} _ {1} \boldsymbol {C} _ {2} (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A}) ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {B} _ {2} \boldsymbol {C} _ {1} (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A}) ^ {- 1}
$$

于是：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {W} (s) = \left( \begin{array}{l l} \boldsymbol {C} _ {1}, & 0 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l l} \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 1} & \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 2} \\ \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 1} & \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 2} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {B} _ {1} \\ 0 \end{array} \right) = \boldsymbol {C} _ {1} \boldsymbol {F} _ {1 1} \boldsymbol {B} _ {1} \\ = C _ {1} \left(s I - A _ {1}\right) ^ {- 1} B _ {1} - C _ {1} F _ {1 1} B _ {1} C _ {2} \left(s I - A _ {2}\right) ^ {- 1} B _ {2} C _ {1} \\ (s \boldsymbol {I} - \boldsymbol {A} _ {1}) ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {B} _ {1} = \boldsymbol {W} _ {1} (s) - \boldsymbol {W} (s) \boldsymbol {W} _ {2} (s) \boldsymbol {W} _ {1} (s) \\ \end{array}
$$

所以有：

$$
\boldsymbol {W} (s) = \boldsymbol {W} _ {1} (s) [ \boldsymbol {I} + \boldsymbol {W} _ {2} (s) \boldsymbol {W} _ {1} (s) ] ^ {- 1} \tag {1.76}
$$

同理也可求得：

$$
\boldsymbol {W} (s) = [ \boldsymbol {I} + \boldsymbol {W} _ {1} (s) \boldsymbol {W} _ {2} (s) ] ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {W} _ {1} (s)
$$

# 1.7 离散时间系统的状态空间表达式

连续时间系统的状态空间方法，完全适用于离散时间系统。类似在连续系统中，从微分方程或传递函数建立状态空间表达式，叫系统的实现。在离散系统中，从差分方程或脉冲传递函数求取离散状态空间表达式，也是一种实现。

设系统差分方程为：

$$
\begin{array}{l} y (k + n) + a _ {n - 1} y (k + n - 1) + \dots + a _ {1} y (k + 1) + a _ {0} y (k) \\ = b _ {n} u (k + n) + b _ {n - 1} u (k + n - 1) + \dots + b _ {1} u (k + 1) + b _ {0} u (k) \tag {1.77} \\ \end{array}
$$

相应地，系统传递函数为：

$$
W (z) = \frac {b _ {n} z ^ {n} + b _ {n - 1} z ^ {n - 1} + \cdots + b _ {1} z + b _ {0}}{z ^ {n} + a _ {n - 1} z ^ {n - 1} + \cdots + a _ {1} z + a _ {0}} \tag {1.78}
$$

实现的任务就是确定一种状态空间表达式：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {x} (k + 1) = \boldsymbol {G} \boldsymbol {x} (k) + \boldsymbol {h} \boldsymbol {u} (k) \\ \boldsymbol {y} (k) = \boldsymbol {c x} (k) + d \boldsymbol {u} (k) \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.79}
$$

在认为两相邻采样时刻， $u(k)$ 不变的条件下，式（1.79）的状态空间表达式也可以用模拟结构图（图1.23）表示。图1.23中 $\pmb{T}$ 代表单位延迟器，类似于连续系统中的积分器。

实现是非唯一的，较简单的实现见图1.24所示的模拟结构图。图中 $a_{i}$ 为已知参数， $h_i$ 为待定常数。以每个延迟器的输出作为一个状态变量，可得：

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-05-05/e98daa7a-7f90-4060-9ef4-3ebaca29cd10/da58a50c549fb5c199078daa5cad552e7eadc1fced07e0a16274e5333d19589c.jpg)



图1.23 离散系统模拟结构图


$$
x _ {1} (k + 1) = x _ {2} (k) + h _ {n - 1} u (k)
$$

$$
x _ {2} (k + 1) = x _ {3} (k) + h _ {n - 2} u (k)
$$

中

$$
\begin{array}{l} x _ {n - 1} (k + 1) = x _ {n} (k) + h _ {1} u (k) \\ x _ {n} (k + 1) = - a _ {0} x _ {1} (k) - a _ {1} x _ {2} (k) - \dots - a _ {n - 1} x _ {n} (k) + h _ {0} u (k) \\ y (k) = x _ {1} (k) + h _ {n} u (k) \\ \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-05-05/e98daa7a-7f90-4060-9ef4-3ebaca29cd10/13eb18e0bbdc49a18429ab7c489814c889a8cb2a64057ed8161c019a533f5fa3.jpg)



图1.24 简单实现的模拟结构图


矢量矩阵形式的离散状态空间表达式为：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {x} (k + 1) = \underbrace {\left( \begin{array}{c c c c c} 0 & 1 & 0 & \dots & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 1 & \dots & 0 \\ \vdots & \vdots & \vdots & \ddots & \vdots \\ 0 & 0 & 0 & \dots & 1 \\ - a _ {0} & - a _ {1} & - a _ {2} & \dots & - a _ {n - 1} \end{array} \right)} _ {G} \boldsymbol {x} (k) + \underbrace {\binom {h _ {n - 1}} {h _ {n - 2}}} _ {h _ {1}} u (k) \\ \mathbf {y} (k) = \underbrace {(1 , 0 , 0 , \cdots , 0)} _ {c} \mathbf {x} (k) + \underbrace {h _ {a}} _ {d} u (k) \\ \end{array}
$$

式中 $h_i$ 的求法，类似于1.4节中式（1.34）求 $\beta_{i}$ 的计算公式，即：

$$
h _ {n} = b _ {n}
$$

$$
h _ {n - 1} = b _ {n - 1} - a _ {n - 1} b _ {n}
$$

： 

$$
h _ {0} = b _ {0} - a _ {0} h _ {n} - a _ {1} h _ {n - 1} - \dots - a _ {n - 1} h _ {1} = b _ {0} - \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {n - 1} a _ {i} h _ {n - i}
$$

多变量离散状态空间表达式为：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {x} (k + 1) = \boldsymbol {G x} (k) + \boldsymbol {H u} (k) \\ \mathbf {y} (k) = \mathbf {C x} (k) + \mathbf {D u} (k) \tag {1.80} \\ \end{array}
$$

# 1.8 时变系统和非线性系统的状态空间表达式

# 1.8.1 线性时变系统

以上讨论的只是定常系统，其特征是它的状态空间表达式中的 $A$ 、 $B$ 、 $C$ 、 $D$ 等矩阵的元素既不依赖于输入、输出，也与时间无关。

线性时变系统有：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {A} (t) = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} a _ {1 1} (t) & a _ {1 2} (t) & \dots & a _ {1 n} (t) \\ a _ {2 1} (t) & a _ {2 2} (t) & \dots & a _ {2 n} (t) \\ \vdots & \vdots & & \vdots \\ a _ {n 1} (t) & a _ {n 2} (t) & \dots & a _ {n n} (t) \end{array} \right) \\ \boldsymbol {B} (t) = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} b _ {1 1} (t) & b _ {1 2} (t) & \dots & b _ {1 r} (t) \\ b _ {2 1} (t) & b _ {2 2} (t) & \dots & b _ {2 r} (t) \\ \vdots & \vdots & & \vdots \\ b _ {n 1} (t) & b _ {n 2} (t) & \dots & b _ {n r} (t) \end{array} \right) \\ \boldsymbol {C} (t) = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} c _ {1 1} (t) & c _ {1 2} (t) & \dots & c _ {1 n} (t) \\ c _ {2 1} (t) & c _ {2 2} (t) & \dots & c _ {2 n} (t) \\ \vdots & \vdots & & \vdots \\ c _ {m 1} (t) & c _ {m 2} (t) & \dots & c _ {m n} (t) \end{array} \right) \\ D (t) = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} d _ {1 1} (t) & d _ {1 2} (t) & \dots & d _ {1 r} (t) \\ d _ {2 1} (t) & d _ {2 2} (t) & \dots & d _ {2 r} (t) \\ \vdots & \vdots & & \vdots \\ d _ {m 1} (t) & d _ {m 2} (t) & \dots & d _ {m r} (t) \end{array} \right) \\ \end{array}
$$

它们的元素有些或全部是时间 $t$ 的函数。

线性时变系统的状态空间表达式为：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} \dot {\boldsymbol {x}} = \boldsymbol {A} (t) \boldsymbol {x} + \boldsymbol {B} (t) \boldsymbol {u} \\ \boldsymbol {y} = \boldsymbol {C} (t) \boldsymbol {x} + \boldsymbol {D} (t) \boldsymbol {u} \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.81}
$$

从高阶线性时变微分方程推演出状态空间表达式的方法，类似于前述线性定常系统。

# 1.8.2 非线性系统

非线性的动态特性是用如下的 $n$ 个一阶微分方程组描述的：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} \dot {x} _ {i} = f _ {i} \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}, \dots , x _ {n}; u _ {1}, u _ {2}, \dots , u _ {r}; t\right), i = 1, 2, \dots , n \\ y _ {j} = g _ {j} \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}, \dots , x _ {n}; u _ {1}, u _ {2}, \dots , u _ {r}; t\right), j = 1, 2, \dots , m \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.82}
$$

用矢量矩阵表示，则为：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} \dot {\boldsymbol {x}} = \boldsymbol {f} (\boldsymbol {x}, \boldsymbol {u}, t) \\ \boldsymbol {y} = \boldsymbol {g} (\boldsymbol {x}, \boldsymbol {u}, t) \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.83}
$$

式中， $f$ 、 $g$ 为矢量函数； $f_{i}$ 、 $g_{j}$ 为 $f$ 、 $g$ 的元素。

如果式（1.82）或式（1.83）中不显含时间 $t$ ，则为时不变非线性系统，而为：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} \dot {\boldsymbol {x}} = f (\boldsymbol {x}, \boldsymbol {u}) \\ \boldsymbol {y} = \boldsymbol {g} (\boldsymbol {x}, \boldsymbol {u}) \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.84}
$$

设 $x_0$ 、 $\pmb{u}_0$ 和 $\pmb{y}_0$ 是满足非线性方程式（1.84）的一组解，即

$$
\dot {\boldsymbol {x}} _ {0} = \boldsymbol {f} \left(\boldsymbol {x} _ {0}, \boldsymbol {u} _ {0}\right)
$$

$$
\mathbf {y} _ {0} = \mathbf {g} \left(\mathbf {x} _ {0}, \mathbf {u} _ {0}\right) \tag {1.85}
$$

如果我们只局限于考察输入 $\pmb{u}$ 偏离 $\pmb{u}_0$ 为 $\delta \pmb{u}$ 时，对应于它， $\pmb{x}$ 也偏离 $\pmb{x}_0$ 为 $\delta \pmb{x}$ ； $\pmb{y}$ 也偏离 $\pmb{y}_0$ 为 $\delta \pmb{y}$ 时的行为，则可以通过对系统的一次近似而予以线性化。为此，将 $\pmb{f}$ 和 $\pmb{g}$ 在 $\pmb{x}_0$ 和 $\pmb{u}_0$ 附近作泰勒级数展开：

$$
\left. \begin{array}{l} f (x, u) = f \left(x _ {0}, u _ {0}\right) + \frac {\partial f}{\partial x} \Bigg | _ {x _ {0}, u _ {0}} \delta x + \frac {\partial f}{\partial u} \Bigg | _ {x _ {0}, u _ {0}} \delta u + \alpha (\delta x, \delta u) \\ g (x, u) = g \left(x _ {0}, u _ {0}\right) + \frac {\partial g}{\partial x} \Bigg | _ {x _ {0}, u _ {0}} \delta x + \frac {\partial g}{\partial u} \Bigg | _ {x _ {0}, u _ {0}} \delta u + \beta (\delta x, \delta u) \end{array} \right\} \tag {1.86}
$$

式中， $\alpha (\delta x, \delta u)$ 、 $\beta (\delta x, \delta u)$ 为关于 $\delta x$ 、 $\delta u$ 的高次项； $\frac{\partial f}{\partial x}$ 、 $\frac{\partial f}{\partial u}$ 分别为矢量 $f(x, u)$ 对矢量 $x$ 和 $u$ 的偏导数； $\frac{\partial g}{\partial x}$ 、 $\frac{\partial g}{\partial u}$ 分别为矢量 $g(x, u)$ 对矢量 $x$ 和 $u$ 的偏导数。

它们分别是 $n \times n, n \times r, m \times n, m \times r$ 维矩阵，其相应定义如下：

$$
\frac {\partial f}{\partial x} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} \frac {\partial f _ {1}}{\partial x _ {1}} & \frac {\partial f _ {1}}{\partial x _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial f _ {1}}{\partial x _ {n}} \\ \frac {\partial f _ {2}}{\partial x _ {1}} & \frac {\partial f _ {2}}{\partial x _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial f _ {2}}{\partial x _ {n}} \\ \vdots & \vdots & & \vdots \\ \frac {\partial f _ {n}}{\partial x _ {1}} & \frac {\partial f _ {n}}{\partial x _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial f _ {n}}{\partial x _ {n}} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \boldsymbol {f}}{\partial \boldsymbol {u}} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} \frac {\partial f _ {1}}{\partial u _ {1}} & \frac {\partial f _ {1}}{\partial u _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial f _ {1}}{\partial u _ {r}} \\ \frac {\partial f _ {2}}{\partial u _ {1}} & \frac {\partial f _ {2}}{\partial u _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial f _ {2}}{\partial u _ {r}} \\ \vdots & \vdots & & \vdots \\ \frac {\partial f _ {n}}{\partial u _ {1}} & \frac {\partial f _ {n}}{\partial u _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial f _ {n}}{\partial u _ {r}} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \boldsymbol {g}}{\partial \boldsymbol {x}} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} \frac {\partial g _ {1}}{\partial x _ {1}} & \frac {\partial g _ {1}}{\partial x _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial g _ {1}}{\partial x _ {n}} \\ \frac {\partial g _ {2}}{\partial x _ {1}} & \frac {\partial g _ {2}}{\partial x _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial g _ {2}}{\partial x _ {n}} \\ \vdots & \vdots & & \vdots \\ \frac {\partial g _ {m}}{\partial x _ {1}} & \frac {\partial g _ {m}}{\partial x _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial g _ {m}}{\partial x _ {n}} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \boldsymbol {g}}{\partial \boldsymbol {u}} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} \frac {\partial g _ {1}}{\partial u _ {1}} & \frac {\partial g _ {1}}{\partial u _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial g _ {1}}{\partial u _ {r}} \\ \frac {\partial g _ {2}}{\partial u _ {1}} & \frac {\partial g _ {2}}{\partial u _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial g _ {2}}{\partial u _ {r}} \\ \vdots & \vdots & & \vdots \\ \frac {\partial g _ {m}}{\partial u _ {1}} & \frac {\partial g _ {m}}{\partial u _ {2}} & \dots & \frac {\partial g _ {m}}{\partial u _ {r}} \end{array} \right)
$$

忽略 $\alpha (\delta x,\delta u)$ 和 $\beta (\delta x,\delta u)$ 高次项，考虑到式（1.85），则式（1.86）的线性化表达式为：

$$
\delta \dot {\boldsymbol {x}} = \dot {\boldsymbol {x}} - \dot {\boldsymbol {x}} _ {0} = \left. \frac {\partial f}{\partial \boldsymbol {x}} \right| _ {\boldsymbol {x} _ {0}, u _ {0}} \delta \boldsymbol {x} + \left. \frac {\partial f}{\partial \boldsymbol {u}} \right| _ {\boldsymbol {x} _ {0}, u _ {0}} \delta \boldsymbol {u}
$$

$$
\delta \mathbf {y} = \mathbf {y} - \mathbf {y} _ {0} = \frac {\partial \mathbf {g}}{\partial \mathbf {x}} \left| _ {\mathbf {x} _ {0}, \mathbf {u} _ {0}} \delta \mathbf {x} + \frac {\partial \mathbf {g}}{\partial \mathbf {u}} \right| _ {\mathbf {x} _ {0}, \mathbf {u} _ {0}} \delta \mathbf {u} \tag {1.87}
$$

令

$$
\left. \frac {\partial f}{\partial x} \right| _ {x _ {0}, u _ {0}} = A; \quad \left. \frac {\partial f}{\partial u} \right| _ {x _ {0}, u _ {0}} = B
$$

$$
\left. \frac {\partial \boldsymbol {g}}{\partial \boldsymbol {x}} \right| _ {x _ {0}, u _ {0}} = \boldsymbol {C}; \quad \left. \frac {\partial \boldsymbol {g}}{\partial \boldsymbol {u}} \right| _ {x _ {0}, u _ {0}} = \boldsymbol {D}
$$

并在式（1.87）中将 $\delta x, \delta u, \delta y$ 这些微增量分别用 $\hat{x}, \hat{u}, \hat{y}$ 表示，则线性化后的表达式就成了一般线性表达式了，即

$$
\dot {\hat {x}} = A \hat {x} + B \hat {u}
$$

$$
\hat {\mathbf {y}} = C \hat {\mathbf {x}} + D \hat {\mathbf {u}} \tag {1.88}
$$

【例1-12】 试求下列非线性系统：

$$
\dot {x} _ {1} = x _ {2}
$$

$$
\dot {x} _ {2} = x _ {1} + x _ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {3} + 2 u
$$

$$
y = x _ {1} + x _ {2} ^ {2}
$$

在 $x_0 = 0$ 处的线性化状态空间表达式。

解 由状态方程和输出方程知：

$$
f _ {1} \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}, u\right) = x _ {2}
$$

$$
f _ {2} \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}, u\right) = x _ {1} + x _ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {3} + 2 u
$$

$$
g \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}, u\right) = x _ {1} + x _ {2} ^ {2}
$$

$$
\left. \frac {\partial f _ {1}}{\partial x _ {1}} \right| _ {x _ {0}} = 0, \left. \frac {\partial f _ {1}}{\partial x _ {2}} \right| _ {x _ {0}} = 1, \left. \frac {\partial f _ {2}}{\partial x _ {1}} \right| _ {x _ {0}} = 1,
$$

$$
\left. \frac {\partial f _ {2}}{\partial x _ {2}} \right| _ {x _ {0}} = (1 + 3 x _ {2} ^ {2}) | _ {x _ {0}} = 1, \left. \frac {\partial g}{\partial x _ {1}} \right| _ {x _ {0}} = 1, \left. \frac {\partial g}{\partial x _ {2}} \right| _ {x _ {0}} = 2 x _ {2} | _ {x _ {0}} = 0
$$

于是

$$
\boldsymbol {A} = \left. \frac {\partial \boldsymbol {f}}{\partial \boldsymbol {x}} \right| _ {\boldsymbol {x} _ {0}} = \left( \begin{array}{l l} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 1 \end{array} \right), \quad \boldsymbol {B} = \left. \frac {\partial \boldsymbol {f}}{\partial \boldsymbol {u}} \right| _ {\boldsymbol {x} _ {0}} = \left( \begin{array}{l} 0 \\ 2 \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
C = \left. \frac {\partial g}{\partial x} \right| _ {x _ {0}} = (1, 0), D = 0
$$

故线性化后的表达式为：

$$
\dot {\hat {\mathbf {x}}} = \left( \begin{array}{l l} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 1 \end{array} \right) \hat {\mathbf {x}} + \left( \begin{array}{l} 0 \\ 2 \end{array} \right) \hat {\mathbf {u}}
$$

$$
\hat {\mathbf {y}} = (1, 0) \hat {\mathbf {x}}
$$

在已知非线性系统的模拟结构图时，建立系统的状态空间表达式是很容易的。例如有图1.25的非线性系统。图中 $\phi_1(\cdot)$ 和 $\phi_{2}(\cdot)$ 表示非线性函数发生器。选取状态变量如图1.25所示，从图易得：

$$
\dot {x} _ {1} = x _ {2}
$$

$$
\dot {x} _ {2} = - \phi_ {1} \left(x _ {1}\right) - \phi_ {2} \left(x _ {2}\right) + u
$$

$$
y = x _ {1}
$$

图1.25 非线性系统模拟结构图


即

$$
\binom {\dot {x} _ {1}} {\dot {x} _ {2}} = \binom {x _ {2}} {- \phi_ {1} (x _ {1}) - \phi_ {2} (x _ {2})} + \binom {0} {1} u = \binom {f _ {1} (x _ {1}, x _ {2})} {f _ {2} (x _ {1}, x _ {2})} + \binom {0} {1} u
$$

$$
y = [ 1, 0 ] x
$$

从已知非线性微分方程求取状态空间表达式则是较复杂的。例如已知:

$$
\ddot {y} + \phi (y) \dot {y} + \varphi (y) = u
$$

它的模拟结构图可表示如图1.26a所示，这里涉及到 $\phi (y)$ 和 $y$ 的乘积。令：

$$
F (y) = \int \phi (y) d y
$$

即

$$
\phi (y) = \frac {\mathrm {d} F (y)}{\mathrm {d} y}
$$

则

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} F (y)}{\mathrm {d} t} = \frac {\mathrm {d} F (y)}{\mathrm {d} y} \cdot \frac {\mathrm {d} y}{\mathrm {d} t}
$$

即

$$
\dot {F} (y) = \phi (y) \dot {y}
$$

$$
